<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对一般量子态进行层析需要指数级资源，但对具有特定结构的态（如低稳定子秩态）可能实现高效层析。此前稳定子扩展度 $\xi(|\psi\rangle)$ 的最佳上界为 $k^{O(k)}$，限制了基于稳定子扩展度的层析协议的适用范围，因此需要改进这一上界。

Method: 首先证明稳定子扩展度可被 $2^{O(k)}$ 界定，将此前 $k^{O(k)}$ 的界改进为单指数形式；随后利用针对稳定子扩展度有界态的层析协议，构造出对稳定子秩为 $k$ 的态的层析协议，达到迹距离 $\varepsilon$。

Result: 稳定子扩展度满足 $\xi(|\psi\rangle)\leq 2^{O(k)}$。基于此，所提出的层析协议可在 $\textsf{poly}(n,2^k,1/\varepsilon)$ 时间内将态层析至迹距离 $\varepsilon$，从而对 $k=O(\log n)$ 实现多项式时间高效层析。

Conclusion: 通过改进稳定子扩展度的界并借助有界稳定子扩展度态的层析协议，实现了低稳定子秩量子态的高效层析，将可高效处理的稳定子秩范围扩展至 $k=O(\log n)$。

Abstract: In this note, we consider tomography of $n$-qubit quantum states $|ψ\rangle$ that are promised to have stabilizer rank $k$ i.e., admit a stabilizer decomposition of $|ψ\rangle = \sum_{i=1}^k c_i |φ_i\rangle$ where each $|φ_i\rangle$ is a stabilizer state. We give a tomography protocol for such states up to trace distance $\varepsilon > 0$ running in $\textsf{poly}(n,2^k,1/\varepsilon)$ time and thus can handle $k=O(\log n)$ efficiently. This follows from first showing that the stabilizer extent $ξ(|ψ\rangle)$, which is the minimal $\ell_1$ norm over the coefficients $\{c_i\}$ of any stabilizer decomposition of $|ψ\rangle$, can be bounded as $ξ(|ψ\rangle) \leq 2^{O(k)}$ and improving upon the previous best bound of $k^{O(k)}$. The protocol is then obtained by utilizing a tomography protocol for states with bounded stabilizer extent.

</details>


### [99] [Quantum Codeword Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03659)
*John Blue,Zachary E. Chin,Vlad S. Oros,Isaac L. Chuang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究通过弱相位相互作用序列传输经典码字的任务，证明在总信号微弱（Qθ→0）时，带有持续量子存储的接收器比仅有经典存储的接收器更可靠，并在二进制单纯码上实现了精确纠错与渐近零解码错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别经弱相位相互作用（每位编码为对传感器量子比特的相同Z旋转）传递给量子传感器寄存器的未知经典码字时，接收端在保留比特间信息方面可能受限于存储类型。核心问题是：在相同量子比特预算Q与旋转角θ下，持续量子存储是否能带来优于经典存储的接收性能。

Method: 将传感器寄存器耦合到持续量子存储；允许辅助量子比特、任意控制幺正与全量子比特纠缠，总预算为Q。针对偶数k、n=2^k−1的[n,k]二进制单纯码，使用Q=Θ((t+1)log n)与θ=2π/(k√(n+1))实现最多t≤(k−2)/4次传输错误的精确纠正；并研究固定概率p<1/16的独立比特翻转下，用Q=O(log n log log n)达到解码错误趋于零。

Result: 当Qθ→0时，耦合持续量子存储的传感器比同Q、θ但仅具经典存储的接收器更可靠，即使传输中发生比特翻转。对上述单纯码，可在t≤(k−2)/4错误下精确纠错；当t=o(√k)时，任何仅经典存储的同类接收器在均匀码字上的平均成功概率趋于零。在独立比特翻转概率p<1/16时，持续量子存储接收器以Q=O(log n log log n)使解码错误趋于零，而仅经典存储的等价接收器平均成功概率消失。

Conclusion: 持续量子存储能在弱信号、受限量子比特预算条件下显著提升经典码字识别与纠错能力；在给定单纯码与噪声模型下，其性能严格优于仅依赖经典存储的接收方案，并可实现渐近可靠的解码。

Abstract: Consider the task of identifying an unknown classical codeword delivered to a register of quantum sensor qubits through a sequence of weak phase interactions that each encode one bit. Suppose that each received bit controls an identical $Z$-rotation of angle $θ$ applied to every sensor qubit; receivers may also use auxiliary qubits within a total qubit budget $Q$, with arbitrary control unitaries and entanglement among all qubits allowed during each reception step. When the total collected signal is weak ($Qθ\rightarrow 0$), we establish that a sensor coupled to a persistent quantum memory can identify the original codeword more reliably than any receiver with the same $Q$ and $θ$ but only classical memory between received bits, even when bit flips occur during transmission. In particular, for a family of $[n,k]$ binary simplex codes with even $k$ and $n=2^k-1$, we achieve exact correction of up to $t\leq(k-2)/4$ transmission errors by coupling the sensor register to a persistent quantum memory of the same size, using $Q = Θ((t+1)\log n)$ and $θ=\frac{2π}{k\sqrt{n+1}}$; for $t=o(\sqrt k)$, every comparable receiver with only classical memory instead has average success probability (over uniformly chosen codewords) tending to zero. Furthermore, under independent bit flips of any fixed probability $p<1/16$, our receiver with persistent quantum memory achieves decoding error tending to zero as $n\to\infty$ using $Q=O(\log n\log\log n)$ total qubits and the same $θ$. In contrast, every otherwise equivalent receiver with only classical memory has vanishing average success probability under the same stochastic error model.

</details>


### [100] [Lie Algebraic Uncertainty Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03661)
*Xiaoli Hu,Naihuan Jing,Jian Zhang,Yunlong Xiao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于协方差的量子不确定性表述，为Lie代数基下的Hermitian与非Hermitian算子给出无限族不确定性关系，并可由代数及表示直接闭式求得最优界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子不确定性关系刻画可观测量的根本不相容性，但迄今仅少数情形已知解析的最优态无关界，缺乏统一且可计算的一般框架。

Method: 引入协方差形式的量子不确定性表述；针对构成Lie代数基的算子，利用代数结构及其表示将原本对量子态的优化替换为直接的代数计算，推导闭式最优界；在每个权空间内分析不确定性的取值。

Result: 得到适用于Hermitian可观测量与非Hermitian算子的无限族不确定性关系；对Lie代数基算子给出闭式最优界；发现同一权空间内不确定性取固定值，使不等式转化为守恒律；表明最小量子不确定性由对易关系及其在量子态上的实现共同决定。

Conclusion: 该框架将不确定性与对称性及表示论相联系，通过底层Lie代数揭示量子信息处理的固有限制，为系统性发现态无关最优不确定性界提供途径。

Abstract: Uncertainty relations express the fundamental incompatibility of quantum observables, yet optimal state-independent bounds are known analytically in only a few cases. Here we introduce a covariance-based formulation of quantum uncertainty that yields an infinite family of relations for both Hermitian observables and non-Hermitian operators. For operators forming a basis of a Lie algebra, we derive the optimal bound in closed form from the algebra and its representation, replacing optimization over quantum states with a direct algebraic calculation. Within each weight space, uncertainty takes a fixed value, turning the inequality into a conservation law. These results show how commutation relations and their realization on a quantum state jointly determine the minimum quantum uncertainty associated with incompatible operators. By connecting uncertainty to symmetry and representation theory, the framework offers a route to uncovering intrinsic limits on quantum information processing through its underlying Lie algebra.

</details>


### [101] [BARC codes: general polynomial framework for coherent-state superposition codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03663)
*Praneel Gore,Davit Aghamalyan,Varun Narasimhachar,Andrew Tanggara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出BARC码代数框架，通过多元复多项式解集对称性约束相干态星座构造玻色量子纠错码，导出最小几何间距上界，并在单模情形给出椭球与六边形码，在纯损耗噪声下基准测试优于球面与cubature码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色量子纠错码需同时抵抗光子增益与光子损耗错误，而现有星座码（如球面码、cubature码）缺乏针对这两类错误的代数化构造方法与几何分离度理论保证。

Method: 建立代数框架：码态为满足多元复多项式系统解集对称性约束的相干态星座有限叠加；约束与光子增益/损耗错误关联，从而施加近似Knill–Laflamme条件；对等权星座点推导最小几何间距上界；单模情形用正交群对称性刻画二次多项式解集，构造显式椭球与六边形BARC码。

Result: 得到等权星座点最小几何间距的上界，反映码的抗噪能力；获得显式椭球型与六边型BARC码族；在纯损耗噪声下以最优恢复的纠缠保真度为指标，代表性实例优于球面码与cubature码。

Conclusion: BARC码提供了一种基于多项式系统对称性的玻色纠错码构造途径，兼具代数结构与几何分离度分析，并在纯损耗噪声基准中展现出竞争优势。

Abstract: We introduce an algebraic framework for constructing a new class of quantum error-correcting codes, bosonic algebraically-restricted constellation (BARC) codes. The code states are finite superpositions of coherent-state constellations constrained by symmetries of solution sets to multivariate complex polynomial systems. The constraints are associated with photon-gain and photon-loss errors, thereby imposing approximate Knill--Laflamme conditions for such errors. For equally-weighted constellation points, we derive upper bounds on the minimum geometric separation between points, indicating how noise-resilient the code is. In the single-mode case, we characterize degree-two polynomial solution sets using an orthogonal group symmetry to obtain explicit ellipsoidal and hexagonal BARC codes. We benchmark representative instances of these families against spherical and cubature codes using the entanglement fidelity with optimal recovery under pure-loss noise.

</details>


### [102] [Optimal kernel functions for linear combination of Hamiltonian simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03669)
*Harriet Apel,Bjorn K. Berntson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文将线性组合哈密顿量模拟（LCHS）的适用核函数类推广为满足自然复分析条件的函数类，建立了相应的LCHS逼近定理；在该类中为每个目标误差构造了唯一使查询代价泛函最小的核函数，给出其渐近展开，并证明该改进在离散化为有限LCU后依然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LCHS为线性非幺正动力学的量子模拟提供了渐近最优框架，其精度由核函数决定。已有工作（Low与Somma）仅在其特定的"最优标度"核函数族内做数值最小化，所得查询代价的渐近系数并非可证明最优。本文旨在扩大可允许核函数类并严格求解最优核函数，从而获得可证明最优的查询代价系数。

Method: 将可允许核函数类推广为满足自然复分析条件的函数类，并建立推广后的LCHS逼近定理；在标准时间无关块编码访问模型下，对每个目标误差ε，在该函数类中显式构造唯一最小化查询代价泛函的核函数；对最优泛函值做渐近分析得到展开式；随后构造具有显式算子误差与LCU次归一化界的谱收敛求积，将结果离散化为有限酉算子的线性组合。理论工具为Hardy空间理论与凸分析。

Result: 查询代价泛函的最小值具有渐近展开 (2e/π)(log(1/ε) − log log(1/ε) + o(1))（ε↓0）；可证明最优的首项系数2e/π相比Low与Somma通过在其最优标度核函数族上数值最小化得到的系数约改进了2.1倍；所构造的谱收敛求积给出显式的算子误差界与LCU次归一化界，证明该渐近改进在离散化为有限LCU后依然保持。

Conclusion: 通过推广核函数类并结合Hardy空间理论与凸分析，本文给出了LCHS框架下可证明最优的查询代价渐近系数2e/π，显著优于此前的经验性数值结果，并且该最优性在可实际实现的有限LCU离散化后仍成立。

Abstract: Linear combination of Hamiltonian simulation (LCHS) provides an asymptotically optimal framework for the quantum simulation of linear non-unitary dynamics. The LCHS approach approximately implements the Peano--Baker propagator through an integral representation determined by a kernel function. We establish an LCHS approximation theorem for a broadened class of admissible kernel functions, defined by natural complex-analytic conditions. In the standard time-independent block-encoding access model, we explicitly construct, for each target error $\varepsilon$, the unique kernel function in this class minimizing the query-cost functional and show that the minimum of this functional has the asymptotic expansion $\tfrac{2\mathrm{e}}π\big(\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}-\log\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}+o(1)\big)$ as ${\varepsilon\downarrow0}$. The provably optimal leading coefficient $2\mathrm{e}/π$ improves by a factor of approximately 2.1 on the coefficient obtained empirically by Low and Somma through numerical minimization over their family of optimally scaling kernel functions. We construct a spectrally convergent quadrature with explicit bounds on operator error and linear combination of unitaries (LCU) subnormalization, proving that this asymptotic improvement persists after discretization into a finite linear combination of unitaries suitable for implementation. Our results are based on Hardy space theory and convex analysis.

</details>


### [103] [Classical Algorithms for Bipartite Quantum Max-Cut on Dense Expanders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03670)
*Stuart Wayland,Zackary Jorquera,Alexandra Kolla*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对稠密平衡二分扩展图上的反铁磁Heisenberg模型（等价于Quantum Max-Cut），给出多项式时间的经典随机算法，可在加性误差ε内估计基态能量和边关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Quantum Max-Cut是研究充分的局部哈密顿量问题，但其二分情形的经典复杂度仍未解决，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在完全二分图的完美匹配上构造马尔可夫链，每个匹配表示单态（singlet）乘积；利用图的扩展性证明多项式步数与采样即足以收敛到基态。

Result: 算法对稠密平衡二分扩展图（包括每个固定p>0的G(n,n,p)以高概率）有效，时间复杂度关于n和1/ε多项式，可估计基态能量及任意基态边关联至加性误差ε。

Conclusion: 该结果提供了二分情形Quantum Max-Cut在稠密扩展图上可经典高效求解的证据，推进了对其经典复杂度的理解。

Abstract: Quantum Max-Cut is a well-studied local Hamiltonian problem for which the classical complexity of the bipartite case remains open. We give a polynomial-time classical randomized algorithm for the spin-$\tfrac12$ antiferromagnetic Heisenberg model, equivalently Quantum Max-Cut, on dense balanced bipartite expanders. This class includes $G(n,n,p)$ with high probability for every fixed $p>0$. The algorithm estimates the ground energy and any ground state edge correlation to additive error $\varepsilon$ in time polynomial in $n$ and $1/\varepsilon$. We construct a Markov chain on perfect matchings of the complete bipartite graph, each matching representing a product of singlets, that converges to the ground state, and use the expansion of the graph to show that polynomially many steps and samples of this chain suffice.

</details>


### [104] [Quantum simulation of field-tunable spin spectroscopy of the quantum magnet Cs2CoCl4 on a trapped-ion quantum computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03671)
*Elias Kokkas,Nora Bauer,Justin Provazza,Mark E. Nowakowski,Kathleen Hamilton,Gilles Buchs,Andrew Sornborger,Travis S. Humble,Ananth Kaushik,Martin Roetteler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用IonQ 36量子比特处理器，以仅420个双量子门电路模拟Cs2CoCl4对应36格点Heisenberg XXZ链的磁激发谱与自旋动力学，结合误差缓解后与DMRG结果高度吻合，验证了量子谱学的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子磁体集体激发谱可检验量子计算机研究强关联物质的能力。但硬件噪声限制了电路深度，因此需探究在电路压缩条件下能保留多少磁性信息，以评估量子处理器进行量子谱学的实用性与精度。

Method: 在IonQ 36量子比特Forte Enterprise处理器上，对描述Cs2CoCl4的36格点横场Heisenberg XXZ链进行模拟。工作流程采用无辅助比特的局域kick协议结合经典张量网络压缩，同时编码基态制备与时间演化；电路仅含420个双量子门，并施加误差缓解方法。

Result: 重建的动态结构因子捕捉到从宽泛的双自旋子连续谱向临界场附近具有磁振子特征的色散分支的演化，并观察到近邻自旋关联的场致符号反转。误差缓解后总谱与DMRG计算高度一致，Pearson相关与结构相似度均高于0.9，归一化均方误差低于0.15；同时与非弹性中子散射实验进行了对比。

Conclusion: 电路压缩与误差缓解使含噪量子处理器能够同时恢复集体激发和局域磁关联，为量子谱学提供了定量基准，并为将该方法推广到更复杂磁性系统奠定基础。

Abstract: The collective excitation spectra of quantum magnets provide a direct test of quantum computers as tools for studying strongly correlated matter. Hardware noise limits the circuit depths available for these calculations, leaving the question of how much magnetic information can survive circuit compression. Using IonQ's 36 qubit Forte Enterprise processor, we simulate the magnetic excitation spectra and spin dynamics of a 36 site Heisenberg XXZ chain describing the quantum magnet Cs2CoCl4 in a transverse field using quantum circuits containing just 420 two qubit gates. The workflow combines an ancilla free local kick protocol with classical tensor network compression, encoding both ground state preparation and time evolution. The reconstructed dynamical structure factors capture the evolution from a broad two spinon continuum toward a dispersive branch with magnon character near the expected critical field. We observe an interesting field driven sign reversal of the nearest neighbor spin correlations which shows a complementary signature of this magnetic evolution. Our error mitigation methods bring the reconstructed total spectra into close agreement with density matrix renormalization group calculations, with Pearson correlation and structural similarity above 0.9 and normalized mean squared error below 0.15. We also perform comparisons with experimental inelastic neutron scattering measurements to connect the recovered excitations to the material's magnetic response. Our results demonstrate that circuit compression and error mitigation allow noisy quantum processors to recover both collective excitations and local magnetic correlations providing a quantitative benchmark for quantum spectroscopy and a basis for extending these methods to more complex magnetic systems.

</details>


### [105] [Ordering-Aware Theory of Trotter Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03672)
*Zhenning Liu,Zhi-Yuan Wei,Michael Foss-Feig,Alexey V. Gorshkov,Andrew M. Childs*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究哈密顿量项排序对乘积公式模拟精度的影响，提出可高效计算的、对排序敏感的Trotter误差上界，并据此优化排序以提升模拟精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘积公式是模拟量子多体动力学的基本工具，但其中哈密顿量项排序这一基本自由度此前基本未被探索，其对模拟精度的影响及可利用性尚不明确。

Method: 针对几何局域哈密顿量，推导了适用于任意高阶乘积公式的、可高效计算且对排序敏感的有限步长Trotter误差上界；利用误差界的局域结构进行排序优化，将长链问题化为加权de Bruijn图上的最小平均权重环问题。

Result: 对平移不变一维链，所推导的界在小系统测试中接近精确的二、四、六阶Trotter误差，在大规模系统上较此前高阶解析界改进了数个数量级；对随机平移不变链，顺序（阶梯状）排序持续优于标准brickwall电路；在电路深度受限时，优化器倾向于波状并行-顺序排序，仍保留大部分相对brickwall电路的精度优势。

Conclusion: 排序可作为理解和改进乘积公式模拟的一个有用自由度，通过误差界驱动的排序优化能够显著提升模拟精度。

Abstract: Product formulas are a basic tool for simulating quantum many-body dynamics, but a fundamental degree of freedom in their application---the ordering of Hamiltonian terms---has remained largely unexplored. Here we systematically study such ordering effects, quantifying their impact on simulation accuracy and exploiting them to design more accurate product-formula simulations. We develop an efficiently computable, ordering-sensitive bound on the finite-step-size Trotter error for geometrically local Hamiltonians for arbitrarily high-order product formulas. For translation-invariant one-dimensional chains, our bounds are close to exact second-, fourth-, and sixth-order Trotter errors in small-system tests, while on large-scale systems they improve on previous higher-order analytical bounds by orders of magnitude. We further use the local structure of the error bounds and estimates to optimize ordering efficiently, reducing the long-chain problem to a minimum-mean-weight-cycle problem on a weighted de Bruijn graph. For random translation-invariant chains, sequential (staircase-like) orderings consistently outperform standard brickwall circuits; when circuit depth is restricted, the optimizer instead favors wave-like parallel-sequential orderings, which preserve much of the accuracy advantage over brickwall circuits. These results establish ordering as a useful degree of freedom for both understanding and improving product-formula simulation.

</details>


### [106] [Quantum Simulation on Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03673)
*Dylan Herman,Jacob Watkins,Guneykan Ozgul,Jiayu Shen,Brandon Augustino,Junhyung Lyle Kim,Shouvanik Chakrabarti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究黎曼流形上薛定谔方程的量子模拟算法：一类基于Laplace-Beltrami算子特征基的全局谱方法，为环面、球面、单纯形、正象限及ℓ_p球等具体流形给出高效量子模拟算法；另一类基于多图卡局部谱方法的相干模拟，原则上适用于任意紧流形，并给出连续与离散化分析。最后应用于优化（测地凸函数上的量子哈密顿下降与实空间绝热算法推广）和物理模拟（含非线性sigma模型等空间离散场论）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黎曼流形上的薛定谔方程在优化与物理模拟中具有重要应用，但其动能项由依赖于度量的Laplace-Beltrami算子给出，缺乏通用且高效的量子模拟算法。作者旨在构造可证明高效的量子算法，覆盖具体流形与一般紧流形两类情形，并展示其在优化和物理系统模拟中的价值。

Method: 第一类为全局谱方法：通过识别到Laplace-Beltrami算子特征基的高效变换，构造显式高效的量子模拟算法，覆盖标准度量环面与球面、Wright-Fisher度量单纯形、对数障碍Hessian度量的截断正象限及其可逆仿射像、Duffy映射诱导度量的ℓ_p球。第二类为多图卡上局部谱方法的相干模拟，先进行连续分析并给出多项式谱截断充分条件，再对常维流形张量积的多项式谱截断进行离散化分析。

Result: 给出了上述具体流形上黎曼薛定谔方程的显式高效量子模拟算法；证明了多图卡局部谱方法在连续情形下多项式谱截断的充分条件，并完成常维流形张量积的离散化分析。在优化方面，给出测地凸函数上量子哈密顿下降的推广与收敛分析，得到球面与单纯形上的显式算法，并推广实空间绝热算法；在物理模拟方面，表明算法可模拟某些空间离散场论，包括非线性sigma模型的一个变体。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了黎曼流形上薛定谔方程量子模拟的系统框架，兼具特定流形上的高效显式算法与面向一般紧流形的通用局部谱方法，并打通了量子优化与量子场论模拟两类应用，为流形上的量子算法设计提供了统一视角。

Abstract: We investigate algorithms for the quantum simulation of the Schrödinger equation on a Riemannian manifold, where the kinetic operator is defined by the Laplace--Beltrami operator corresponding to the metric. Our first algorithms are based on a global spectral method based on the identification of an efficient transform to the eigenbasis of the Laplace--Beltrami operator. We use this method to provide explicit, efficient, quantum simulation algorithms for the Riemannian Schrödinger equation on tori and spheres with their standard metrics, simplices with the Wright--Fisher metric, truncated positive orthants and their invertible affine images with the log-barrier Hessian metric, and $\ell_p$ balls with a metric induced by the Duffy map. Our second algorithm is based on a coherent simulation of local spectral methods on multiple charts, and is in principle applicable to any compact manifold. We first analyze this algorithm in the continuum and derive conditions under which a polynomial spectral cutoff suffices. We also provide a discretization analysis of a polynomial spectral cutoff for tensor-products of constant-dimensional manifolds. Finally, we consider applications of these methods to optimization and physical simulation. For optimization, we provide results including a generalization and convergence analysis of Quantum Hamiltonian Descent for geodesically convex functions that leads to explicit algorithms on the sphere and simplex, and a Riemannian generalization of the Real-Space Adiabatic Algorithm. For physical simulation, we show that our algorithms can simulate certain spatially discretized field theories, including a variant of the nonlinear sigma model.

</details>


### [107] [Efficient capacity-achieving entanglement generation with application to pure-loss Bosonic channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03674)
*Avijit Mandal,Christophe Piveteau,Henry D. Pfister,Joseph M. Renes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用极化码构造了一种高效纠缠生成方案，对一大类信道达到相干信息；对可退化信道（含截断纯损耗玻色信道）达到量子容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子通信在噪声信道上的速率极限虽已确立，但能达到该极限的高效编码方案极少，与经典信道中极化码和LDPC码均能高效达到经典容量形成鲜明对比。

Method: 构造高效纠缠生成方案，基于两类经典输入量子输出（CQ）信道的极化码：输出可对易的情形用经典极化译码算法；输出为纯态预示混合的情形用带量子消息的置信传播（BPQM）算法。对可退化信道（含截断纯损耗玻色信道）可达量子容量。

Result: 该方案对广泛的信道类达到相干信息，对其中可退化信道达到量子容量；这是首个明确且可高效译码、能达到纯损耗玻色信道能量约束量子容量的协议。

Conclusion: 极化码可有效用于量子信道编码，为达到量子容量提供了显式、可高效译码的构造，扩展了经典极化码思想至量子通信场景。

Abstract: While the ultimate limits on the rate of quantum communication over noisy channels are well-established, very few efficient coding schemes achieving these limits are known. This contrasts sharply with transmitting classical data over classical channels, where polar codes and low-density parity-check codes both efficiently achieve the classical capacity of any noisy channel. In this work we construct an efficient entanglement generation scheme which achieves the coherent information for a broad class of channels. For degradable channels in the class, which includes truncated versions of the pure loss Bosonic channel, the resulting scheme achieves the quantum capacity. The construction makes use of polar codes for two kinds of classical-input, quantum-output (CQ) channels: those with commuting outputs and those whose outputs are heralded mixtures of pure states. The former case is amenable to decoding by classical polar decoding algorithms, the latter by the belief propagation with quantum messages (BPQM) algorithm. To our knowledge, this construction is the first explicit and efficiently decodable protocol that achieves the energy-constrained quantum capacity of the pure-loss Bosonic channel.

</details>


### [108] [How I Learned to Stop Worrying and Love the Redfield Equation: Completely-Positive Resummation of Non-Markovian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03676)
*Greg Kaplanek,Jason Pollack*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了Redfield方程（TCL2）与同一二阶微扰映射的Magnus（指数化）重求和，构造了CPTP resolvent重求和以说明重求和的非唯一性，并给出弱耦合判据，保证非马尔可夫Redfield演化仍接近CPTP映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Redfield方程是开放量子系统动力学最简单的微扰描述之一，但其作为近似的地位微妙：微扰论逐阶固定约化动力学，而求解Redfield方程得到的特定重求和不一定保持完全正性。作者希望厘清Redfield演化何时以及为何可靠，即便它高度非Lindblad型。

Method: 将Redfield演化与同一二阶微扰映射的指数化（Magnus）重求和进行比较，后者在广泛物理条件下是完全正且保迹（CPTP）的；并构造一个简单的CPTP resolvent重求和以展示同一微扰映射重求和的非唯一性；提出一个弱耦合判据，并在有限维（量子比特）系统中用两个例子加以说明。

Result: Redfield与Magnus方案在二阶一致，但在更高阶通过时间排序修正而不同；构造的CPTP resolvent重求和证明重求和并非唯一；所提弱耦合判据保证即使非马尔可夫的Redfield演化仍接近CPTP映射，并在两个量子比特例子中得到验证。

Conclusion: 研究澄清了Redfield演化在高度非Lindbladian时仍可保持可靠的条件与原因，强调了重求和的非唯一性以及完全正性在微扰框架中的微妙地位。

Abstract: The Redfield equation with time-dependent coefficients (or TCL2 equation) gives one of the simplest perturbative descriptions of open quantum system dynamics derived from a microscopic theory. Its status as an approximation is nevertheless subtle: while perturbation theory fixes the reduced dynamics order-by-order in the system-environment coupling, solving the Redfield equation yields a particular resummation that need not preserve complete positivity. Here we compare Redfield evolution with an exponentiated (Magnus) resummation of the same second-order perturbative map, which is completely positive and trace preserving (CPTP) for a broad class of physical conditions. We also construct a simple CPTP resolvent resummation, illustrating the non-uniqueness of resummations of the same perturbative map. The Redfield and Magnus prescriptions agree to second order but differ through time-ordering corrections at higher orders. We exhibit a weak-coupling criterion which guarantees that Redfield evolution, even when non-Markovian, remains close to a CPTP map. We illustrate the criterion in two examples, focusing on finite-dimensional (qubit) systems. Our results clarify when, and why, Redfield evolution can remain reliable even when it is highly non-Lindbladian.

</details>


### [109] [Exact Recovery for Non-Abelian Surface Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03677)
*Alison Warman,Nathanan Tantivasadakarn,Sakura Schafer-Nameki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了基于任意有限群 G 的量子双重 D(G) 的非阿贝尔拓扑表面码的精确恢复问题，提出正交错误基、规范固定方案以及确定性的电荷/磁通转移恢复电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非阿贝尔拓扑表面码相比阿贝尔码具有更强的纠错潜力，但其错误基存在冗余（闭合环路错误与稳定子相关），导致恢复困难。现有恢复方案多针对阿贝尔群，缺乏对任意有限群（尤其非阿贝尔群）的精确、确定性恢复协议。

Method: 1) 确定由群乘法算符和不可约表示算符构成的正交错误基；2) 针对闭合格点或对偶格点上闭合环路的冗余，引入规范固定，在码空间上得到完备正交错误基；3) 以预先给定的中性可纠错误簇为输入，构造电荷转移电路和磁通转移电路，将错误转移到辅助比特上并投影消除。

Result: 得到了码空间上完备且正交的错误基，并构造出电荷与磁通转移电路，实现对任意有限群 G（包括非阿贝尔群）表面码的精确、确定性恢复。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了适用于任意有限群非阿贝尔拓扑表面码的确定性精确恢复协议，扩展了拓扑量子纠错的适用范围。

Abstract: We study exact recovery for non-Abelian topological surface codes based on the quantum double $D(G)$ of any finite group $G$. We determine an orthogonal error basis, that is comprised of group multiplication and irreducible representation operators. Whenever the errors are supported on closed loops on the lattice or dual lattice, there is a redundancy in the basis, due to stabilizers. To remedy this, we introduce a gauge-fixing that results in a complete and orthogonal error basis on the code space. Assuming as input predetermined, neutral correctable error clusters, we construct charge and flux transfer circuits that move the errors onto ancillas that then get projected out. This is an exact and deterministic recovery protocol, applicable to surface codes for any finite, in particular non-Abelian, group $G$.

</details>


### [110] [Low-Overhead Quantum Error Correction with Boundary-Connected Planar Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03682)
*Oscar Higgott,Hasan Sayginel,Francisco J. H. Heras,Zhiyang He,Tomas Jochym-O'Connor,Andrew W. Senior,Lei M. Zhang,Thomas Edlich,James S. Spencer,Matt McEwen,Craig Gidney,Johannes Bausch,Pushmeet Kohli,Hartmut Neven*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出将量子处理器划分为平面模块并通过非局域连接构建模块化双曲/色彩码，在电路级模拟下显著降低物理量子比特开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面表面码虽应用最广，但物理量子比特开销巨大，实现实用量子计算机需要克服这一瓶颈。

Method: 将量子处理器划分为可制造的欧几里得平面模块，在模块间引入稀疏、静态的边界连线；基于新半双曲码族构造模块化双曲表面码和色彩码；设计模块化症状提取电路，并使用神经网络与匹配解码器进行电路级模拟；开发基于码自同构的容错行走电路，并协同设计低权重、高对称逻辑基与约小4.5倍的模块化解码器系统。

Result: 电路级模拟显示物理量子比特开销比表面码减少十倍以上，即使模块接缝错误率升高；外推更大系统可得到编码率k/n=1/16、距离d≥22的模块化码，超过[[288,12,18]]双格双变量自行车码的速率与距离，开销相对表面码降低超30倍。

Conclusion: 模块化存储可作为通用容错架构中的高密度存储单元，在不牺牲平面模块制造优势的前提下实现高码率码的高效率。

Abstract: Realizing practical quantum computers requires quantum error correction, but the most widely implemented approach, the planar surface code, demands a substantial physical qubit overhead. Here, we demonstrate that partitioning quantum processors into manageable, flat modules with non-local inter-module connections provides a natural solution. By wiring sparse, static boundary connections between Euclidean planar modules, we construct modular hyperbolic surface and color codes, based on new families of semi-hyperbolic codes. Using modular syndrome extraction circuits and efficient neural network and matching decoders, our circuit-level simulations show that this modular memory design can reduce the physical qubit overhead by tenfold or more compared to the surface code, even under elevated inter-module seam error rates. Projecting to larger system sizes, we find modular codes with encoding rate $k/n=1/16$ and distance $d\geq 22$, exceeding both the rate and distance of the $[[288,12,18]]$ two-gross bivariate bicycle code while using mostly nearest-neighbor gates within planar modules, and implying an over $30\times$ overhead reduction relative to surface codes. We develop fault-tolerant walking circuits for implementing logic via code automorphisms, which we co-design with low-weight, highly symmetrical logical bases and a modular extractor system that is $\approx 4.5\times$ smaller than the code itself. Combining these methods, our modular memory can be used as dense storage in a universal fault-tolerant architecture, unlocking the efficiency of high-rate codes without sacrificing the fabrication advantages of planar modules.

</details>


### [111] [Single-Shot Error Correction at Optimal Spacetime Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03684)
*Kishor Bharti,Tobias Haug,Derek Khu,Andrew Tanggara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：在长程连通性与快速可靠经典处理支持下，使用量子Tanner码和显式含噪纠错电路及高效解码，可将擦除信道的时空成本下界 Ω(S(K+log(S/ε))) 以仅增加对数级开销的代价实际达到，且每步成本与码长线性相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作给出独立擦除信道下存储K个逻辑量子比特S步、误差≤ε所需时空成本的下界，且存在匹配构造，但该构造假设纠错是理想的。本文旨在去掉这一理想化假设，给出在显式含噪电路和高效解码下仍能达到相同标度的构造，从而说明可靠性只会带来所有存储量子比特共享的对数级开销。

Method: 采用量子Tanner码；每个存储步包括一轮综合征提取，随后进行固定数量的并行经典解码步骤，将残余误差降至足以保证后续故障可纠正的水平，且不依赖存储规模、存储时间或目标精度。方法适用于足够弱但一般的电路噪声，包括综合征提取过程中的故障与相关故障。

Result: 在硬件支持长程量子比特连通性及快速、可靠经典处理的条件下，构造实现了与理想纠错相同的标度，且计入所有态制备、门、测量与等待位置的成本；对独立擦除信道，该构造与已知最优下界在常数因子内匹配；每步保持指数小的失败概率，同时每步成本与码长线性相关。

Conclusion: 可靠性仅引入由所有存储量子比特共享的对数级开销；相同分析还可推广到某些常数深度逻辑Clifford操作。

Abstract: Recent work established that, for independent erasures, storing $K$ logical qubits for $S$ time steps with error at most $\varepsilon$ requires a spacetime cost of $Ω(S(K+\log(S/\varepsilon)))$. A matching construction was also given, but assumes ideal error correction. Here we show that the same scaling can be achieved with an explicit noisy error-correction circuit and efficient decoding, counting all state-preparation, gate, measurement, and wait locations, provided that the hardware supports long-range qubit connectivity and fast, reliable classical processing. Thus, reliability adds only a logarithmic overhead shared by all stored qubits. The construction works for sufficiently weak but otherwise general circuit noise, including faults during syndrome extraction and correlated faults, and for independent erasures it matches the best known lower bound up to constant factors. We attain this scaling using quantum Tanner codes, with each memory step consisting of one round of syndrome extraction followed by a fixed number of parallel classical decoder steps that reduce the residual error enough to keep later faults correctable, regardless of memory size, storage time, or target accuracy. Together, this gives exponentially small failure probability per step while keeping the cost of each memory step linear in the code size. The same analysis also extends to certain constant-depth logical Clifford operations.

</details>


### [112] [Efficient Block Encoding of Structured Hamiltonians by Separating Where and What](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03686)
*Alessandro Summer,François Jamet*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于支持几何的置换-作用-反置换容错块编码电路，用CSWAP网络将选定支持搬到固定目标寄存器，使SELECT的T计数随系统规模而非项数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化哈密顿量的容错块编码中，SELECT的非Clifford成本通常随项数增长，需要降低T计数并避免依赖平移对称性或因式分解系数。

Method: 构造置换-作用-反置换电路；对两站点支持，在地址控制的站点转置网络中建立并达到最小CSWAP数；编译Pauli项为8SN+O(log N)个T门，使用O(log N)置换工作量子比特；引入跨接CSWAP以保持并修复目标作用期间的临时AND。

Result: T计数随系统规模增长，S由支持几何决定，最近邻S=1，全对全S=3/2；修复为Clifford时，匹配的正反CSWAP对的T计数减半，代价为一个保留工作量子比特；海森堡环约降低3倍，五轨道Anderson杂质模型约降低1.7倍，且块编码归一化和峰值辅助比特数可比。

Conclusion: 该方案在不依赖平移对称性或因式分解系数的情况下，显著降低结构化哈密顿量块编码的T计数，并在具体模型上验证了大幅T门节省。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant block encodings of structured Hamiltonians can reduce their non-Clifford cost in SELECT by selecting where an operator acts separately from what is applied. We construct permute-act-unpermute circuits whose CSWAP networks exploit the support geometry: a selected support is brought to a fixed target register, a shared local circuit acts there, and the permutation is undone. For supports of fixed size, the $T$ count of SELECT then grows with the system size rather than with the number of terms, without requiring translational symmetry or factorised coefficients. For two-site supports, we establish and attain the minimum CSWAP count within address-controlled networks of site transpositions. Compiled for Pauli terms, these circuits use $8SN+O(\log N)$ $T$ gates with $O(\log N)$ permutation work qubits, where $N$ is the number of system qubits and $S$ is determined by the support geometry, with $S=1$ for nearest-neighbour interactions and $S=3/2$ for all-to-all pairs. We also introduce a bridged CSWAP that retains and repairs a temporary AND across the target action. When the repair is Clifford, it halves the $T$ count of a matched forward-inverse CSWAP pair at the cost of one retained work qubit. For a Heisenberg ring, the complete block-encoding query reduces the $T$ count by a factor approaching 3 as the system size grows. For a five-orbital Anderson impurity model, which combines nearest-neighbour and all-to-all support geometries, the reduction is about 1.7 at large bath sizes. Both comparisons use the lowest-cost compiled baselines considered, at unchanged block-encoding normalisation and comparable peak ancilla counts.

</details>


### [113] [Universal Bounds for Out-of-Distribution Unitary Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03690)
*Joachim Favre,Armando Angrisani,Zoë Holmes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出基于输入分布一阶与二阶矩的量子动力学分布外学习框架，用"偏置"与"表达力"刻画集合，控制任意纯态训练/测试分布间的泛化，并给出一系列正反结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是回答未知酉动力学在一族态上的作用能在多大程度上揭示其在其他态上的行为，即酉动力学的分布外泛化问题，并厘清酉性在该泛化中的独特作用。

Method: 方法上构建一般性框架，基于输入分布的一阶与二阶矩定义集合的偏置与表达力，用以分析任意纯态训练分布与测试分布之间的泛化；并据此证明若干结构性结论，同时推导紧的 product-to-Haar 界，并将此前的局部加扰结果作为松散特例纳入。

Result: 主要结果包括：仅学习酉在随机实态上的作用即可学到其在随机复态上的作用；无偏纯态集合的张量积保持表达力；单量子比特风险与偏置无关；此前局部加扰结果被作为松散特例恢复，并得到紧的 product-to-Haar 界；还给出若干 no-go 结果与对特定信道学习的受限泛化界。

Conclusion: 结论强调酉性在实现分布外泛化中具有独特作用；该框架统一并推广了已有结果，同时揭示了学习更一般信道时的根本限制。

Abstract: How much can the action of an unknown unitary dynamics on one family of states reveal about its action elsewhere? We develop a general framework for out-of-distribution quantum dynamics learning based on the first and second moments of the input distribution. These define a notion of the bias and expressivity of an ensemble, which together control generalization between arbitrary pure-state training and testing distributions. Our framework shows that only studying the action of a unitary on random real states can learn its action on random complex states, that tensor products of unbiased pure-state ensembles preserve expressivity, and that single-qubit risks are independent of bias. It also recovers earlier locally scrambling results as loose special cases and yields tight product-to-Haar bounds. We conclude with a series of no-go results and limited generalization bounds for learning certain channels, which further serve to underscore the distinctive role of unitarity in enabling out-of-distribution generalization.

</details>


### [114] [An operational characterization of finite-dimensional quantum theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03694)
*Lionel J. Dmello,Xiangling Xu,Marc-Olivier Renou,David Gross*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过对若干双体关联加以"在迭代传态下稳定"的约束，证明满足这些关联的唯一概率论就是量子理论，从而在有限维情形下实现量子理论的自检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学基础研究的一个核心目标是找出刻画物理理论的"操作性约束"。Bell不等式刻画了经典概率论，Tsirelson界则是量子力学的第一步。本文关注其对偶问题：能否证明量子理论所预言的所有关联都是真正可实现的，即为量子理论本身提供认证。

Method: 构造有限数目的双体关联，要求满足两个条件：(1) 某一概率论理论能实现这些关联；(2) 这种实现方式在迭代传态（iterated teleportation）下保持稳定。对 (C^d)^{⊗n} 情形，条件(1)可用 poly(d,n) 个测量设置加以验证；条件(2)可理解为对应传态步数 N 的一系列层级检验。

Result: 满足上述两个条件的唯一概率论理论是量子理论，即有限维量子理论在某种意义上是可自检验的。特别地，可以认证出比任何已直接观测到的都更大的 Bell 不等式违背。

Conclusion: 有限维量子理论具有自检验性质；通过引入迭代传态下的稳定性要求，可将量子理论从所有概率论理论中唯一地刻画出来，并由此认证超越现有观测的 Bell 违背。

Abstract: A key goal in the foundations of quantum mechanics is to identify operational constraints characterizing physical theories. Bell inequalities do so for classical probability theory, while Tsirelson's bound provides a first step for quantum mechanics. Here, we address the dual question: Can one certify that all correlations predicted by quantum theory are actually realizable? In this work, we construct a finite number of two-body correlations such that the only probabilistic theory that (1) realizes them, and (2) does so in a way that is stable under iterated teleportation, is quantum theory. For $(\mathbb{C}^d)^{\otimes n}$, Condition (1) can be verified using $\operatorname{poly}(d,n)$ measurement settings. Condition (2) may be understood as a hierarchy of tests, one for each number $N$ of teleportation steps. There is thus a sense in which finite-dimensional quantum theory can be self-tested. In particular, one can certify the existence of Bell inequality violations larger than any that have been directly observed.

</details>


### [115] [Maximum-Entropy Extension of Quantum Correlation Functions from Short Real-Time Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03696)
*Filippo Maria Gambetta,Sabrina Yue Wang,Raul A. Santos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Burg最大熵原理的量子关联函数时间序列延拓与谱密度重构方法，通过将关联函数与平稳随机过程的协方差序列建立联系，实现从短时数据高精度重构谱密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子关联函数的谱给出准粒子和集体激发信息，但获得足够谱分辨率所需的长时演化对经典和量子模拟均难以实现，因此需要从短时数据外推关联函数并重构谱密度。

Method: 利用关联函数与平稳随机过程协方差序列的联系，应用Burg最大熵原理求取与已测关联函数值相容的最小假设延拓，对应全极点自回归模型，其系数求解Yule-Walker方程；方法推广至标量和矩阵值关联函数。

Result: 所得延拓按构造为正半定，保证谱的正性并自动满足总谱权重求和规则；适用于结构化或连续谱；矩阵情形可用更多已测关联函数换取模拟时间，在含噪短时数据下实现准确延拓与谱重构。

Conclusion: 该方法为短时量子模拟数据提供了有原则的谱重构途径，兼顾正性约束与噪声鲁棒性，并可扩展至矩阵关联函数场景。

Abstract: Correlation functions are central to understanding the behavior of quantum systems, with their spectra giving direct access to quasiparticle and collective excitations. However, the long-time evolution required for sufficient spectral resolution is challenging to reach for both classical and quantum simulations. In this work, we introduce a principled approach for extending time series of quantum correlation functions and reconstructing their spectral densities, based on their connection with covariance sequences of stationary stochastic processes. This connection enables us to apply Burg's maximum-entropy principle to find the least-committal extension compatible with the measured values of the correlation function, which corresponds to an all-pole autoregressive model whose coefficients solve a set of Yule--Walker equations. The resulting extension is positive semi-definite by construction, guaranteeing the positivity of the spectrum and the automatic satisfaction of its total spectral weight sum rule. The approach applies to both scalar and matrix-valued correlation functions, including those with highly structured or continuous spectra. The generalization to the matrix case makes it possible to trade simulation time for additional measured correlation functions, enabling accurate time-series extension and spectral reconstruction from short-time data, even in the presence of noise.

</details>


### [116] [On The Complexity of Redundancy-Free Quantum Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03697)
*Matthew B. Hastings,Alexander Schmidhuber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究无冗余哈密顿量的复杂性，证明其配分函数逼近在 β²d 处呈转变：小 β²d 可经典高效求解，大 β²d 为 NP-hard；并给出制备热场双态的子指数量子算法，以及基态能量估计的 QMA 完备性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此类哈密顿量（任意含奇数出现次数项乘积迹为零）自然出现在 Hamiltonian Decoded Quantum Interferometry (DQI) 中，该框架把量子态制备难度拆为经典解码与制备无冗余哈密顿量热场双态两步。理解其硬度既有独立意义，也有助于刻画 Hamiltonian DQI 中态制备步骤的复杂度。

Method: 引入"反交换玻璃"(anticommutation glass)，其中阻挫纯粹源自反交换关系；以反交换图度 d 与逆温 β 的组合量 β²d 作为复杂度标度参数，构造小 β²d 下逼近配分函数的经典算法，并用归约证明大 β²d 下逼近配分函数为 NP-hard；基于 Feiguin-Klich 哈密顿量给出制备热场态的子指数量子算法及多项式算法的部分结果；并将无冗余哈密顿量基态能量估计归约为 QMA 完备问题。

Result: 问题在比一般哈密顿量二次更低的温度下变易，复杂度依赖 β²d；小 β²d 存在高效经典配分函数逼近算法，大 β²d 时逼近配分函数 NP-hard；给出小 β²d 下制备热场态的子指数量子算法；Feiguin-Klich 哈密顿量可导出在度 d 交互图上、β≲1/d 时制备一般哈密顿量热场双态的多项式时间算法；无冗余哈密顿量基态能量估计为 QMA 完备。

Conclusion: 无冗余哈密顿量的热力学与基态问题展现出以 β²d 为标度的复杂度相变结构，兼具可解与难解区域，并在态制备与基态能量估计上建立了量子复杂度类刻画（子指数量子算法、QMA 完备性）。

Abstract: We consider a class of redudancy-free Hamiltonians, which are those where the trace vanishes for any product of Hamiltonian terms in which at least one term appears an odd number of times. This includes Hamiltonians which are sums of products of Paulis with no relations between them. These Hamiltonians arise naturally in Hamiltonian Decoded Quantum Interferometry, which separates the hardness of preparing quantum states into two steps: a classical decoding step, and a step preparing the thermofield double state of such a redundancy-free Hamiltonian.
  We focus on the hardness of these Hamiltonians, both for intrinsic interest and to understand the complexity of the state preparation step in Hamiltonian DQI. We present several results showing that the problem becomes easy at quadratically lower temperature than for a general Hamiltonian, with the complexity at given inverse temperature $β$ depending on $β^2 d$, where $d$ is the degree of the anticommutation graph. We give an efficient classical algorithm for small $β^2 d$ to approximate the partition function, while for large $β^2 d$ we show that approximating the partition function is NP-hard. To this end, we introduce what we call an anticommutation glass, where frustration arises purely from anticommutation relations. We give a subexponential time quantum algorithm to prepare the thermofield state for small $β^2 d$, and present some results toward a polynomial time algorithm based on the Feiguin-Klich Hamiltonian. As a result of possible independent interest, we show that this Hamiltonian leads to a polynomial time algorithm for preparing thermofield double states of general Hamiltonians on an interaction graph of degree $d$ for $β\lesssim 1/d$. Finally, we show that estimating the ground state energy of redundancy-free Hamiltonians is QMA-complete.

</details>


### [117] [Exponential lower bounds on the fermionic Gaussian rank of magic states and the bosonic coherent state rank of Fock states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03699)
*Oliver Reardon-Smith*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了费米子高斯分解 |M⟩^{⊗k} 至少需要 Ω(1.4^k) 项，并将该下界推广到近似秩及相干态秩，回答了相关猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学经典模拟算法的超多项式复杂度依赖于magic态在free态下的分解项数（rank）。尽管已知稳定子秩和费米子高斯秩有指数上界，但稳定子秩的最佳下界仅为二次，费米子高斯秩仅有常数界，理论上存在巨大空白。

Method: 针对费米子线性光学的标准magic态|M⟩（4量子比特可消费实现swap门），证明其k重张量积的任意费米子高斯分解至少含Ω(1.4^k)项；将方法推广至δ-近似秩（下界为精确下界乘1-δ²）；并将精确秩结果推广至任意k个固定宇称非高斯态乘积。此外，用同一方法证明n模玻色Fock态（模式j含m_j个玻色子）的相干态边界秩恰为∏(1+m_j)，并给出近似相干态秩下界。

Result: 费米子高斯精确秩下界为Ω(1.4^k)，近似秩下界为(1-δ²)倍该值；相干态边界秩等于∏_j(1+m_j)，并得到近似相干态秩下界。

Conclusion: 该工作显著提升了费米子高斯秩的下界（从常数到指数），回答了Ref.[1]关于相干态边界秩的猜想，并展示了所用方法在量子态分解秩研究中的广泛适用性。

Abstract: Recent algorithms for classical simulation of quantum mechanics have runtime whose superpolynomial component is given by a linear dependence on the number of terms required to write large tensor products of certain "magic" states as superpositions of "free" states (which may be stabilizer states, fermionic Gaussian states or others). Surprisingly little is known about the number of terms in such decompositions, called ranks (e.g. the stabilizer rank, fermionic-Gaussian rank etc.). For complexity theoretic reasons they are expected to grow exponentially in the number of tensor factors but, while exponential upper bounds are known for both the stabilizer and fermionic-Gaussian rank of magic states; the best known lower bounds on the stabiliser rank are quadratic, and no bounds on the fermionic-Gaussian rank are known beyond fixed constants. In this work we prove that any fermionic Gaussian decomposition of $\lvert M\rangle^{\otimes k}$ consists of $Ω(1.4^k)$ terms. Here $\lvert M\rangle$ is the most standard magic state for fermionic linear optics: the $4$ qubit state that may be consumed to implement a swap gate. We also prove essentially matching bounds on the $δ$-approximate rank of the same state, lower bounding it by the same quantity that bounds the exact rank, multiplied by a factor of $1-δ^2$. Our results on exact fermionic Gaussian rank apply directly to any product of $k$ fixed parity non-Gaussian states, although the same is not true of the approximate rank. Finally, we prove that the coherent state border rank of an $n$-mode bosonic Fock state with $m_j$ bosons in mode $j$ is exactly $\prod_{j}(1+m_j)$, answering a conjecture of Ref. [1] and obtain lower bounds on the approximate coherent state rank given by the same quantity multiplied by a function of the fidelity of the approximation, emphasizing the broad applicability of the method we employ.

</details>


### [118] [Scalable Passive QRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03700)
*Siddhartha Jain,Alexander M. Dalzell,Connor T. Hann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可扩展、被动的物理QRAM实现蓝图，首次实现每次查询polylog N的能量开销，并在原理上证明其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现物理QRAM操作通常面临可扩展性与能量开销问题，作者希望构造一种被动（无需主动控制）且能量高效的方案。

Method: 使用预制的、时间无关的4-局域哈密顿量进行演化；该哈密顿量含O(N)项，每个量子比特最多参与4项。对n量子比特地址（对应N=2^n存储位），通过哈密顿量演化实现QRAM查询。

Result: 每次QRAM查询能量开销为O(log N)，为已知首个达到polylog N能量的构造；每次查询运行时间为O(log^2 N)。

Conclusion: 该方案以被动、可扩展方式实现物理QRAM，原理上可行，并在能量与时间开销上取得优势。

Abstract: We present a blueprint for implementing the physical QRAM operation in a scalable, passive manner, proving its feasibility in principle. Our construction is passive because for an $n$-qubit address over $N = 2^n$ memory bits the energy cost per QRAM query is $O(\log N)$, and to our knowledge it is the first construction to use $\operatorname{polylog} N$ energy. The runtime for each query is $O(\log^2 N)$. It is achieved by evolving a pre-manufactured time-independent 4-local Hamiltonian with $O(N)$ terms where every qubit participates in at most 4 terms.

</details>


### [119] [Unitary complexity in polynomial space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03705)
*William Kretschmer,Ewin Tang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明若量子承诺存在，则要么酉合成问题无多项式时间解，要么 BPP ≠ NEXP；核心是一个关于 unitaryPSPACE 的通用引理，并给出 unitaryP 与 unitaryPSPACE 的新定义及其鲁棒性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨量子承诺存在性这一假设的复杂性理论代价：证明其无条件成立至少需要解决两个长期未决的复杂性理论问题之一，从而揭示量子承诺与酉合成、经典复杂性下界之间的深刻联系。

Method: 提出 unitaryP 与 unitaryPSPACE 的新定义，涵盖擦除垃圾与允许垃圾两类实现（并证明二者可互相视为特例）；通过一个通用引理刻画 unitaryPSPACE 中酉变换的合成性质，并结合经典 oracle 与 NEXP 搜索 oracle 进行分析；同时给出 unitaryPSPACE 的等价刻画（矩阵元素可在多项式空间内任意精度计算）。

Result: 证明若量子承诺存在，则要么酉合成问题不存在多项式时间解，要么 BPP ≠ NEXP；通用引理表明 unitaryPSPACE 中每个酉要么相对任何经典 oracle 都无法高效合成，要么可用 NEXP 搜索 oracle 高效合成；推出某些涉及 unitaryPSPACE 的 oracle 分离会意味着 NC ≠ NP 等经典下界突破；证明 unitaryPSPACE 可泛型擦除垃圾，但该性质相对酉 oracle 可证不成立。

Conclusion: 量子承诺存在性无法被无条件证明而不触及深层复杂性开放问题；新定义解决了先前工作的核心概念问题并带来简洁证明，且具有多方面鲁棒性，为酉复杂性类的研究提供了更稳固的基础。

Abstract: We show that if quantum commitments exist, then either there is no polynomial-time solution to the unitary synthesis problem, or $\mathsf{BPP} \neq \mathsf{NEXP}$. Thus, showing unconditionally that quantum commitments exist would require answering at least one of two longstanding open questions in complexity theory. We prove our main result as a consequence of a more general lemma, which shows that every unitary in $\mathsf{unitaryPSPACE}$ either cannot be synthesized efficiently relative to any classical oracle, or can be synthesized efficiently with an oracle for $\mathsf{NEXP}$ search problems. Our lemma has other noteworthy consequences, including that certain oracle separations involving $\mathsf{unitaryPSPACE}$ would imply breakthrough classical lower bounds such as $\mathsf{NC} \neq \mathsf{NP}$.
  Along the way, we propose new definitions for the unitary complexity classes $\mathsf{unitaryP}$ and $\mathsf{unitaryPSPACE}$. Our changes address the biggest conceptual issues with definitions suggested in prior work, and lead to elegant proofs. We study both implementations that erase garbage and implementations that allow it, because we cannot rule out the possibility that the two definitions differ. Nevertheless, we show that both definitions can be viewed as special cases of each other. We also showcase many other ways in which our definitions are robust. For example, we show that $\mathsf{unitaryPSPACE}$ has an equivalent characterization as the set of unitary transformations whose entries can be computed to arbitrary precision in polynomial space. Consequently, we deduce that $\mathsf{unitaryPSPACE}$ can generically erase garbage, a result that provably fails relative to unitary oracles.

</details>


### [120] [Quantum estimation, channel orders, and private capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03706)
*Christoph Hirche*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文通过二元估计与measured-χ²散度，将现有熵型信道序与"零私密容量但可激活传私"的构造联系起来，给出完整比较序与正则化比较序的差异机制，并在qutrit、Werner–Holevo等信道族中确定私密容量、反退化阈值及精确补容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反例表明零私密容量既不蕴含反退化性，两个零私密容量信道联合仍可传私；而现有熵型信道序虽能界定容量，却未解释这些新构造，需建立信道序与该激活现象之间的定量联系。

Method: 以二元估计的最小均方误差为桥梁，用measured-χ²散度刻画信道序；引入积分表示，说明qubit扩展上的measured-χ²可恢复quantum-χ²，从而实现从完整measured-χ²序到完整quantum-χ²、相对熵及less-noisy序的过渡；用Hermitian平滑的measured-χ²序刻画signed lift存在性；将每块长的环境支配转化为有限码可靠性—保密性界。

Result: 完整比较可阻止借助反退化辅助信道的激活，而仅正则化比较不行；在qutrit示例基础上，对一类含噪Werner–Holevo信道证明该稳定性，确定尖锐的私密容量与反退化阈值，并在非退化区间计算精确补容量；将Pauli半擦除激活推广至所有1/2≤p<1，并在新的四能级族得到相同区间；参考辅助的measured-χ²见证表明这些激活构造缺乏完整比较性。

Conclusion: 信道序的完整性与正则化差异是决定私密信息激活能否借助反退化辅助信道发生的关键；measured-χ²/Hermitian平滑序为判定signed lift与激活机制提供了统一判据。

Abstract: Recent examples have shown that zero private capacity need not imply antidegradability and that two channels with zero private capacity can nevertheless transmit private information together. Existing entropic channel orders compare what a receiver and its environment can learn and thereby bound capacities. We connect these orders to the recent constructions through binary estimation, whose minimum mean-square error is governed by measured $χ^2$ divergence. A new integral representation shows how measured $χ^2$ on a qubit extension recovers quantum $χ^2$. It lets us pass from complete measured-$χ^2$ ordering to complete quantum-$χ^2$, relative-entropy, and less-noisy ordering. Zhu and Wang used a signed lift to show that their qutrit channel has zero private capacity. We show that Hermitian-smoothed measured-$χ^2$ ordering characterizes when such lifts exist, even with a quantum reference. Environmental dominance in binary estimation at every blocklength yields a finite-code reliability--secrecy bound. These results explain why complete comparison prevents activation with antidegradable helpers whereas regularized comparison alone does not. Building on that qutrit example, we establish this stability for a range of noisy Werner--Holevo channels, determine sharp private-capacity and antidegradability thresholds, and compute exact complementary capacities in a nondegradable range. By contrast, we extend Pauli half-erasure activation to all $1/2\le p<1$ and establish the same range for a new four-level family. Reference-assisted measured-$χ^2$ witnesses show why these activating constructions lack complete comparison.

</details>


### [121] [How to Build Pseudorandom Unitaries in Microcrypt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03711)
*Aditya Gulati,Dakshita Khurana,Kabir Tomer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文首次给出黑盒分离证据，表明在经典随机预言机下存在伪随机酉（PRU），同时不存在量子可计算单向函数，即 PRU 不蕴含量子单向函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 Kretschmer、Qian 和 Tal（STOC 2025）仅给出较弱的经典预言机结果。该文希望在更强对手模型下强化这一分离，证明 PRU 的存在性与量子可计算单向函数相互独立，并探索可用随机预言机启发式实现的简洁构造。

Method: 首先证明五个独立随机对角相位层与 Hadamard 变换交错构成的酉 $U=F_5 H F_4 H F_3 H F_2 H F_1$ 是强 PRU，能抵抗对酉及其逆、转置、复共轭的自适应受控访问。随后将相位层用经典随机预言机 $O$ 实现，并证明该构造对具有相干访问能力的统一 BQP 敌手仍安全，即使敌手可相干访问经典输入 $QMA^{PH^O}$ 判定问题的合适布尔完备化。

Result: 构造在经典随机预言机 $O$ 下保持安全，可抵抗能反演（甚至量子可计算）单向函数、并解决相对 $O$ 的高效可验证单向谜题的 QMA 辅助敌手。这构成黑盒分离，显著强化了 Kretschmer–Qian–Tal 的结果。

Conclusion: PRU 不蕴含量子可计算单向函数。该构造电路简单高效、仅查询随机函数，提示可通过随机预言机启发式走向具体实现。

Abstract: We provide the first evidence relative to a classical oracle that pseudorandom unitaries (PRUs) can exist without quantum-computable one-way functions.
  To obtain this result, we first prove that five independent random diagonal phase layers interleaved with Hadamard transforms \[ U=F_5HF_4HF_3HF_2HF_1 \] form a strong PRU with security under adaptive, controlled access to the unitary, its inverse, transpose, and complex conjugate.
  Our main result is that, when the phase layers are implemented using a classical random oracle O, the construction remains secure against uniform BQP adversaries with coherent access to suitable Boolean completions of classical-input $QMA^{PH}^{O}}$ decision problems. Such an adversary can invert (even quantum-computable) one-way functions and solve efficiently verifiable one-way puzzles defined relative to O. Our result therefore provides classical-oracle evidence that PRUs do not imply quantum-computable one-way functions, in the form of a black-box separation.
  We substantially strengthen the classical-oracle result of Kretschmer, Qian, and Tal (STOC 2025) by obtaining PRUs secure against these QMA-aided adversaries. Our construction also consists of a simple, efficient circuit that queries a random function, suggesting a route to concrete implementation via the random-oracle heuristic.

</details>


### [122] [SpiderCSS: Scalable Fault-Tolerant CSS State Preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03714)
*Boldizsár Poór,Andrey Boris Khesin,Sarah Meng Li,Benjamin Rodatz,John van de Wetering,Richie Yeung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 SpiderCSS，一个可扩展的编译流程，利用 ZX 演算与容错等价重写，为任意 CSS 码生成高度优化的容错逻辑态制备电路，兼具 CNOT 最小性与可证明容错性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑态制备是大规模容错量子计算的关键基础操作，但现有可扩展或启发式方法生成的电路在深度、量子比特占用和逻辑错误率方面仍有较大优化空间，且容错性验证代价高昂。

Method: 从 CSS 态的理想化规范出发，使用 ZX 演算和容错等价重写构造能制备目标态且保持原理想 ZX 图容错性的图；再由该图通过模块化构造生成容错 CSS 态制备电路，该构造在每个组件内部及组件间连接方式上均实现 CNOT 最小化；因仅使用容错等价重写，电路无需昂贵验证即可证明容错。

Result: 在距离最高达 15 的多种 CSS 码上进行蒙特卡洛模拟，SpiderCSS 能高效扩展，所构造电路相比现有可扩展与启发式方法显著降低深度和量子比特占用，同时逻辑错误率平均降低 33.7%，接受率提升 9.5%。

Conclusion: SpiderCSS 提供了一种可扩展、可证明容错且高度优化的 CSS 逻辑态制备电路编译方案，在电路规模与逻辑错误率等指标上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant preparation of logical states is a critical primitive for the realization of large-scale fault-tolerant quantum computing. This paper introduces SpiderCSS, a scalable compilation pipeline that generates highly-optimized, fault-tolerant state preparation circuits for arbitrary Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) codes. Starting from an idealized specification of a CSS state, we use the ZX-calculus and fault-equivalent rewrites to construct a diagram that prepares the given state while preserving the fault tolerance of the initial ideal ZX-diagram. From this diagram, SpiderCSS constructs a fault-tolerant CSS-state preparation circuit using a modular construction that is CNOT-minimal both within each component and also in the way these components are connected together. By only using fault-equivalent rewrites, the final circuit is provably fault-tolerant without needing expensive verification. Monte Carlo simulations across a variety of CSS codes up to distance 15 show that SpiderCSS scales efficiently and constructs circuits with substantially reduced depth and qubit footprint compared to existing scalable and heuristic methods, while simultaneously achieving an average decrease of 33.7% in logical error rate and an increase of 9.5% in acceptance rate.

</details>

### [123] [Noncommutative Marcus-Ree inequality and Erdős channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02294)
*Repana Devendra,Chaman Kumar Sahu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Marcus-Ree关于双随机矩阵Frobenius范数的不等式推广到量子信道，定义了Erdős信道，并在混合酉信道、条件期望、RCDS信道及量子比特情形下给出结构与分类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典矩阵论中Marcus与Ree（1959）证明了双随机矩阵的Frobenius范数平方不超过其最大对角和，等号成立者称为Erdős矩阵。作者希望建立该理论的非交换（量子）类比，研究酉量子信道（双随机矩阵的非交换对应）上的相应不等式与等号刻画，并揭示量子情形与经典情形的本质差异。

Method: 1）在M_d上对混合酉信道证明Marcus-Ree不等式的量子版本，并构造反例说明一般酉信道不成立；2）定义Erdős信道（取等号者）；3）证明到任意酉C*-子代数的迹保持条件期望是Erdős信道，且在相同Choi秩的信道中局部孤立；4）引入量子RCDS信道（所有支撑置换具有相同对角和的双随机矩阵之非交换类比），证明结构定理并刻画量子RCDS Erdős信道；5）给出Choi秩2的Erdős信道的显式形式（至多相差酉等价），并证明存在不可数多个酉等价类；6）刻画任意Choi秩的混合Weyl-酉信道中的Erdős信道；7）对量子比特情形完全分类Erdős信道；8）限制到对角信道以回收经典结果。

Result: 证明了混合酉信道上的量子Marcus-Ree不等式，并发现一般酉信道存在反例；条件期望为Erdős信道且局部孤立；建立了量子RCDS信道的结构定理并刻画其Erdős信道；给出Choi秩2 Erdős信道的显式形式，并证明其酉等价类不可数（与经典情形不同）；刻画了混合Weyl-酉信道中的Erdős信道；对量子比特证明恰有四个酉等价类，分别由恒等信道和Pauli信道的均匀平均表示；对角信道情形回收Marcus-Ree不等式、RCDS双随机矩阵结构及Erdős矩阵刻画。

Conclusion: 该工作系统建立了双随机矩阵Erdős理论的量子推广，揭示了量子情形下Erdős信道分类的丰富性（如不可数多个酉等价类）与经典情形的根本差异，同时保持了对角限制下的经典一致性。

Abstract: Unital quantum channels are the noncommutative analogues of doubly stochastic matrices. In 1959, Marcus and Ree showed that the squared Frobenius norm of a doubly stochastic matrix is at most its maximal diagonal sum. We prove a quantum version of this inequality for mixed unitary channels on $\mathbb{M}_d$ and give an example showing that it fails for general unital channels. As in the classical case, we call the channels attaining equality as Erdős channels.
  We show that the trace-preserving conditional expectation onto any unital $C^*$-subalgebra of $\mathbb{M}_d$ is an Erdős channel. It is also locally isolated among channels of the same Choi rank. Next, we introduce quantum RCDS channels. These are the noncommutative analogue of doubly stochastic matrices whose supported permutations all have the same diagonal sum. We prove a structure theorem for them and use it to characterize the quantum RCDS Erdős channels. We give an explicit form, up to unitary equivalence, for the Erdős channels of Choi rank $2$, and further show that there are uncountably many unitary equivalence classes of Erdős channels unlike the classical case. We also characterize the Erdős channels among mixed Weyl-unitary channels of any Choi rank.
  For qubits, we prove that there are exactly four unitary equivalence classes of Erdős channels and they are represented by the identity channel and uniform averages of Pauli channels. Finally, restricting to the diagonal channels, our results recover the Marcus--Ree inequality, the structure of RCDS doubly stochastic matrices, and the characterization of Erdős.

</details>


### [124] [Stratification of the space of density matrices via invariant theory and Uhlmann's connection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02773)
*Howy Jordan,Markus J. Pflaum,Gerd Rudolph*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文严格证明了密度矩阵空间的秩分解构成Whitney (b)-正则分层，并将Uhlmann联络几何化地处理为分层向量丛上的分层联络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息理论文献中虽有"将密度矩阵态空间按等秩分解可得Whitney分层"的断言，但一直缺乏严格证明；同时Uhlmann联络作为混合态绝热和乐（在纯态上退化为Berry联络）也需要恰当的几何框架来刻画。

Method: 运用不变量理论（invariant theory）的方法，证明矩阵代数的秩分解构成Whitney意义下的(b)-正则分层；并构造分层向量丛与分层联络的框架，用以几何化地描述Uhlmann联络。

Result: 证明了$n\times n$密度矩阵空间的等秩分解确实是一个(b)-正则Whitney分层；表明Uhlmann联络可视为分层向量丛上的分层联络；附录给出了分层纤维丛与分层联络这一新概念的自足阐述。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子信息中长期作为断言使用的秩分层陈述提供了严格的数学证明，并把Uhlmann联络纳入分层几何框架，所提出的分层联络概念具有独立的学术价值。

Abstract: The space of $n\times n$ density matrices is the full state space of an $n$-level quantum system. While it has been asserted in the literature on quantum information theory that decomposing this state space into equirank pieces yields a Whitney stratification, rigorous proofs have remained absent. Using methods from invariant theory, this work provides a proof that the rank decomposition of the matrix algebra constitutes a (b)-regular stratification in the sense of Whitney. Furthermore, we demonstrate how the Uhlmann connection - which describes the adiabatic holonomy of mixed states and reduces to the Berry connection on pure states - can be geometrically treated as a stratified connection on a stratified vector bundle. Finally, our appendix provides a self-contained exposition of stratified fiber bundles and the new concept of stratified connections, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [125] [The monatomic limit of polyatomic gases as concentration of the internal-energy measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03060)
*Tommaso Ruggeri*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究RATIONAL扩展热力学中多原子气体单原子极限的奇异性，证明当内态密度指数取a=(D-5)/2时，归一化平衡内能分布在D→3时集中于I=0，为Dirac处方及其一阶修正提供严格依据；分析ET14中动压Π的容许范围与熵边界角，指出负动压可行但固定负值不能逼近单原子态，强调相容初值必要性；相对论情形下给出固定动量下的条件伽马分布、本构积分、精确能量恒等式及Synge能量的一阶修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rational Extended Thermodynamics框架下，多原子气体向单原子气体过渡（D→3）的极限常呈奇异行为，Dirac处方缺乏严格数学依据；同时经典ET14中动压符号与边界、熵的边界角行为、以及相对论内能分布与本构关系需要更精确刻画，以说明相容初值条件与能量修正。

Method: 对分子内能采用内态密度φ(I)=I^a并取a=(D-5)/2，研究归一化平衡分布在D→3的集中现象；在经典ET14中由正定性推出动压范围-p<Π<(D-3)p/3，并分析精确熵在Π=0处的边界角；在相对论情形下证明固定动量时内能服从条件伽马分布，借此直接计算本构积分并推导精确能量恒等式与Synge能量的一阶修正。

Result: 证明了D→3时归一化平衡内能分布集中于I=0，从而严格支撑Dirac处方及其一阶修正；ET14中动压满足-p<Π<(D-3)p/3，熵在Π=0出现边界角，负动压可容许但固定负值在指定总能量下不逼近单原子态；相对论中内能为固定动量下的条件伽马分布，本构积分可直接给出，并得到精确能量恒等式与Synge能量的一阶修正。

Conclusion: 多原子气体的单原子极限具有本质奇异性，必须借助Dirac型集中与修正项才能正确描述；负动压虽理论上允许，但与非平衡初值和总能量约束相关，因此需要相容初始数据；相对论内能的伽马分布表述为计算本构关系和能量修正提供了直接而严格的工具。

Abstract: In Rational Extended Thermodynamics, the monatomic limit of polyatomic gases is often singular. For the internal-state density $φ(I)=I^a$, with $a=(D-5)/2$, we prove that the normalized equilibrium distribution of the molecular internal energy $I$ concentrates at $I=0$ as $D\to3$, providing a rigorous justification of the Dirac prescription and its first-order correction. In classical ET14, the internal-mode energy differs from equilibrium by $-3Π/2$; positivity yields $-p<Π<(D-3)p/3$, while the exact entropy develops a boundary corner at $Π=0$. Negative dynamic pressures remain admissible, but fixed negative values do not approach the monatomic state with the prescribed total energy. This explains the need for compatible initial data. In the relativistic case, the internal energy is conditionally gamma distributed at fixed momentum; the constitutive integrals follow directly, and we derive an exact energy identity and the first correction to the Synge energy.

</details>


### [126] [On the Cauchy Problem for the Sawada-Kotera Equation: Soliton resolution conjecture, Painlevé transcendents and asymptotic stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03097)
*Zheng-Kang Huang,Shou-Fu Tian*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究具有连续谱与有限离散谱的可容许初值下Sawada-Kotera (SK)方程在不同时空区域的长时渐近行为，证明了孤子分解猜想，给出了由F-XVIII Painlevé超越函数主导的渐近式，并建立了N-孤子解的渐近稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于SK方程长时渐近理论的结果仅限于纯连续谱情形，无法处理孤子与辐射共存的一般可容许数据；本文旨在将长时渐近理论推广到离散谱与连续谱共存的情形，并解决谱原点处奇异结构及稳态点合并等困难。

Method: 核心工具包括：(1) 新的亚纯Riemann-Hilbert框架，通过完整的六点极点轨道引入离散谱，同时保持余因子解析性和谱原点处的奇异结构；(2) 轨道化、区域相关的极点约化机制与\bar{\partial}-非线性最速下降法相结合，用于选择各区域贡献的孤子并将其与连续辐射分离；(3) 利用正则修正SK问题与Miura映射处理谱原点处合并的稳态点。

Result: 在不同时空区域导出了SK方程的长时渐近展开；证明了孤子分解猜想；渐近行为由F-XVIII Painlevé超越函数支配；并建立了N-孤子解的渐近稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作将SK方程的长时渐近理论从纯连续谱情形推广到孤子与辐射共存的一般情形，所提出的亚纯Riemann-Hilbert框架与区域相关极点约化方法为处理含离散谱的完全可积系统渐近分析提供了有效途径。

Abstract: We derive long-time asymptotics in different space-time regions for the Sawada-Kotera (SK) equation with admissible data involving continuous spectrum and finite discrete spectrum. Our result proves the soliton resolution conjecture, yields the asymptotics governed by the F-XVIII Painlevé transcendent, and establishes the asymptotic stability of \(N\)-soliton solutions. More importantly, this extends the long-time asymptotic theory to the coexistence of solitons and radiation, whereas previous results were restricted to the purely continuous spectrum. One key ingredient in our argument is a new meromorphic Riemann-Hilbert framework that incorporates the discrete spectrum through complete six-point pole orbits while preserving cofactor analyticity and the singular structure at the spectral origin. A second ingredient is the combination of an orbitwise, region-dependent pole reduction mechanism and the \(\bar{\partial}\)-nonlinear steepest descent method, which allows us to select the solitons contributing in each region and separate them from the continuous radiation. Our framework also resolves the coalescing stationary points at the singular spectral origin through the regular modified SK problem and the Miura map.

</details>


### [127] [Conformal Bootstrap for surfaces with boundary in Liouville CFT. Part II: spectral resolution and bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03539)
*Colin Guillarmou,Rémi Rhodes,Baojun Wu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文是紧致带边Liouville共形场论共形bootstrapping证明的第二部分，聚焦半环带半群的谱理论并导出bootstrapping公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Part 1建立的Segal公理与粘合性质基础上，需要刻画半环带半群的谱结构，以获得边界态空间的分解并导出一般关联函数的分块表示，从而完成对Liouville CFT的共形bootstrapping证明。

Method: 将半环带半群的生成元与边界Hamiltonian等同，利用散射方法建立其谱分解，得到边界态空间关于不可约Virasoro表示的直积分分解，由边界谱测度控制；结合边界形变的Ward恒等式，将一般关联函数表达为沿切割曲线的谱参数积分，被积函数为体与边界结构常数及相应共形块的乘积。

Result: 给出了边界态空间在不可约Virasoro表示上的直积分分解；关联函数以谱参数积分形式表示，被积函数由体/边界结构常数与共形块构成；结果为共形块的解析性与对称性以及映射类群在共形块空间上的表示构造提供支撑。

Conclusion: 该工作完成了带边紧致曲面上Liouville CFT共形bootstrapping证明的关键谱理论部分，导出了bootstrapping公式，并服务于共形块解析性质与映射类群表示的相关研究。

Abstract: This paper is the second part of the proof of the conformal bootstrap for Liouville conformal field theory on compact surfaces with boundary. It is devoted to the spectral theory of the half-annulus semigroup and to the resulting bootstrap formula. Building on the Segal axioms and gluing properties established in Part~1~\cite{GRW1}, we identify the generator of the half-annulus semigroup with the boundary Hamiltonian and establish its spectral decomposition by scattering methods. This yields a direct-integral decomposition of the boundary state space into irreducible Virasoro representations, governed by the boundary spectral measure. Combining this decomposition with Ward identities for boundary deformations, we express general correlation functions as integrals over spectral parameters attached to cutting curves, with integrands given by products of bulk and boundary structure constants and the corresponding conformal blocks. These results also underpin the analyticity and symmetry properties of conformal blocks~\cite{GRSSbloc}, and contribute to the construction of mapping class group representations on spaces of conformal blocks~\cite{PapierBlocs}.

</details>


### [128] [Virasoro Conformal Blocks and modular functor from Liouville CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03540)
*Guillaume Baverez,Colin Guillarmou,Antti Kupiainen,Rémi Rhodes,Yuxiao Xie*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在中心荷 c>25 情形下给出黎曼面上 Virasoro 共形块的全局构造，证明其为全纯线丛的全纯截面并满足 Ward 恒等式，同时刻画了共形块的空间结构、度量与映射类群的投影酉表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Virasoro 共形块是二维共形场论与模空间几何的核心对象。此前对共形块的构造多为局部或形式性的，缺乏在 Teichmüller 空间上的全局解析构造与整体性质（全纯性、Ward 恒等式、映射类群作用等）的统一处理，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 以中心荷 c>25 为条件，在亏格 g、含 m 个标记点的黎曼面 Teichmüller 空间上全局构造 Virasoro 共形块；利用带标记的裤子分解（pair of pants decomposition）将其与 L² 空间相联系，并借助 DOZZ 结构常数构造测度；进而证明其满足 Ward 恒等式并研究 Moore-Seiberg 移动下的变换。

Result: 证明共形块是全纯线丛的全纯截面并满足编码共形不变性的 Ward 恒等式；对三点的球面，共形块空间为一维；对给定标记黎曼面及标记裤子分解，共形块空间一般为无穷维 Hilbert 空间，在含 DOZZ 结构常数的测度下同构于 L²(R_+^{3g-3+m})；该空间承载映射类群的投影酉表示，且对每个 Moore-Seiberg 移动存在相应的酉同构。

Conclusion: 本文为 c>25 的 Virasoro 共形块提供了全局、解析且具有几何意义的构造，统一揭示了其全纯性、共形不变性、Hilbert 空间结构以及映射类群与 Moore-Seiberg 移动下的酉性质。

Abstract: In this article, we give a global construction of the Virasoro conformal blocks on Teichmüller space of Riemann surfaces of genus $g$ with $m$ marked points, when the central charge is $c>25$. We prove that they are global holomorphic sections of a holomorphic line bundle and they satisfy the Ward identity, which encodes their conformal invariance. For the sphere with $3$ points, the space of conformal blocks is one-dimensional. For a given marked Riemann surface and a marked pair of pants decomposition, it is in general an infinite dimensional Hilbert space, isomorphic to $L^2(\R_+^{3g-3+m})$ with respect to some measure involving the DOZZ structure constants. We show that it carries a projective unitary representation of the mapping class group. More generally, there are unitary isomorphisms for each Moore-Seiberg move, changing a marked pair of pants decomposition into another one.

</details>

### [129] [Interaction-induced period shift of the Kohn oscillation in a parity-time-symmetric harmonic trap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02984)
*Gaoqing Meng,Mingshu Zhao*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究简谐势中玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体在宇称-时间对称增益-损耗梯度下的质心动荡，发现无相互作用时质心频率不变且刚性移动，有相互作用时保护失效并出现周期随增益强度的二次增长，周期轨道可延续至与高激发态共振的临界增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简谐势中凝聚体质心以陷阱频率振荡且与相互作用无关（Kohn定理），而无相互作用时宇称-时间对称增益-损耗梯度不改变该频率并导致刚性平移与原子数周期调制。作者希望探究引入相互作用后这一保护机制是否仍然成立，以及增益-损耗如何通过原子数调制影响云团宽度并反馈至质心运动。

Method: 在简谐势中采用宇称-时间对称增益-损耗梯度，分别分析无相互作用与有相互作用两种情况下的质心动荡；用五变量矩模型描述云团宽度与质心运动，并在Thomas-Fermi区域解析导出周期随增益强度的二次增长系数，通过数值延续Kohn振荡的周期轨道至临界增益。

Result: 无相互作用时质心频率不变，基态宽度的云团被刚性平移且原子数周期性调制；有相互作用时保护丧失，调制原子数驱动云团宽度，宽度再反作用于质心。小增益下周期随增益强度二次增长，Thomas-Fermi区域该系数仅由云团尺寸决定。从Kohn振荡延续的周期轨道可追踪到临界增益，此时与陷阱高激发态共振；计算范围内临界增益随振荡幅度和云团尺寸增大而减小。五变量矩模型几乎直到临界增益都能重现轨道上的周期与云团宽度。

Conclusion: 相互作用破坏了宇称-时间对称增益-损耗梯度对质心振荡频率的保护，通过原子数调制的宽度-质心耦合导致周期随增益强度二次增长；周期轨道存在临界增益并与高激发态共振，临界增益随振幅与云团尺寸增大而降低，五变量矩模型能较好地刻画直至临界增益的动力学。

Abstract: The center of mass of a Bose--Einstein condensate in a harmonic trap oscillates at the trap frequency independently of the interactions. Without interactions a parity-time-symmetric gain-loss gradient leaves this frequency unchanged, and a cloud of the ground-state width is translated rigidly while the atom number is periodically modulated. With interactions the protection is lost, because the gain and the loss change the number of atoms; the modulated atom number drives the width of the cloud, and the width acts back on the center of mass. At small gain the period grows quadratically with the gain strength, with a coefficient that in the Thomas--Fermi regime is set by the size of the cloud alone. The family of periodic orbits continued from the Kohn oscillation can be followed up to a critical gain, where it comes into resonance with highly excited states of the trap, and over the computed families this critical gain decreases with the amplitude of the oscillation and with the size of the cloud. A five-variable moment model reproduces the period and the width of the cloud along the orbits nearly up to the critical gain.
